_source line 4374 · 2026-05-22T06:32:14.828603+00:00_

Perfect. I've successfully retrieved all nine TeX source files from the paper project. Here's the complete content of each file:

## 1. abstract.tex

The abstract describes a study on detecting the generation depth of LLM-generated text. Key findings include differential depth response (DDR)—where successive re-generation compresses where text sits in distributional space while leaving how it's shaped relatively unchanged. The paper demonstrates that NLL location and lexical diversity drift monotonically with depth (Cohen's d=0.76–1.17) while core distributional shape features remain near-stationary (d≤0.10). A 7-feature ensemble achieves binary F1=0.694 under leave-one-generator-out evaluation, with an end-to-end classifier reaching 3-class F1=0.905.

## 2. introduction.tex

The introduction contextualizes the problem: 7–17% of recent scientific publications contain LLM-modified content. The paper introduces depth fingerprinting—classifying text by ordinal generation depth (gen-0/gen-1/gen-2+)—and decompose compression into structurally distinct components across eight base-model generators from five families (0.6B–8B parameters). Three main contributions are listed: empirical decomposition (DDR), task formulation with evaluation suite, and scope characterization.

## 3. related_work.tex

This section covers four subsections:
- **AI-Generated Text Detection**: Reviews detection literature including statistical tests, zero-shot scoring, and learned classifiers, noting that prior work moves beyond binary but taxonomizes by source/production mode rather than recursive depth
- **Model Collapse and Iterative Generation**: Discusses self-consuming models and compression during retraining, contrasting with the paper's focus on inference chains
- **Vocabulary Diversity and Stylometry**: Connects declining lexical diversity in LLM text to classical stylometric measures
- **Data Quality and Provenance**: Discusses scalable data curation and watermarking approaches

<details open><summary>🔧 Tool call — mcp__agent-ml-research__review_reviewer_post_to_director</summary>

```json
{
  "text": "\n## Reviewer 2 — Round 1 审稿报告\n\n**论文**: *Depth Fingerprinting: Classifying Recursive Generation Depth in Base-Model LLM Text*\n**审查侧重**: 新颖性 + 完整性 + 写作质量\n\n---\n\n## 总体评分\n\n**Overall**: 3.0 (Findings)\n**一句话总结**: 扎实的 empirical study，DDR 分解是有价值的发现，但 base-model-only scope 严重限制了实际影响力，方法论贡献偏薄，\"fingerprinting\" framing 高于实际分辨率。\n\n## 官方维度打分（ACL ARR Review Form）\n\n| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |\n|------|------|----------|\n| Soundness | 3.5/5 | 实验设计严谨（LOGO、bootstrap CI、TOST 等效检验），但存在 scope 与 motivation 错位、术语混淆等问题 |\n| Excitement | 2.5/5 | 新 task formulation 有意思，但观察性贡献为主，方法本身（7 特征 XGBoost）缺乏方法论创新 |\n| Overall Assessment | 3.0/5 | Findings 可接收——empirical finding 有价值，但 scope 限制和 framing 问题阻止其进入主会议 |\n| Reproducibility | 4/5 | 超参数、代码承诺、计算预算均有说明，数据管线清晰；但代码未随稿件提供 |\n| Confidence | 4/5 | 熟悉 AI text detection 和 LLM 评估领域，仔细检查了主要 claim |\n\n---\n\n## Must-Fix 问题（影响接收决定）\n\n**N1** [critical] Scope 与 Motivation 之间存在根本性错位\n\n- 具体位置：§1 Introduction（\"An estimated 7–17% of recent scientific publications already contain substantial LLM-modified content\"），§5 Discussion（corpus management, contamination estimation），Limitations\n- 问题：论文的 motivation 来自真实世界的 LLM 文本污染检测（科学出版物、训练语料），但这些场景中的 LLM 输出几乎全部来自 **instruction-tuned models**。然而实验限定在 base model，且 Appendix k 明确显示 instruction tuning 导致 depth signal 显著衰减（F1 从 0.762 降至 3-class F1 by 3.7–19.9 pp，entropy mean Spearman ρ 从正变负）。这造成了论文叙事的核心矛盾：读者被引导相信这项工作对训练数据污染检测有意义，但实验证据实际上表明该方法在最需要的场景（instruction-tuned model 输出）中不work。\n- 为什么影响接收：这不是一个可以在 Limitations 中一笔带过的边界条件——它动摇了论文的整个 motivation chain。reviewer 和 AC 会问：\"如果对 instruct 模型无效，这个 task 的实际价值在哪里？\"\n- 建议修改方向：(1) 重构 framing：将论文重新定位为对 base-model recursive generation 的 **分析性/科学性研究**（understanding how recursive sampling changes distributional properties），而非面向应用的检测工具；(2) 或者提供 instruct model 上的正面结果（即使是 attenuated），证明核心 signal 仍然可利用。\n\n**N2** [critical] \"Fingerprinting\" 标题与 3-class 分辨率严重不匹配\n\n- 具体位置：Title，Abstract（\"depth fingerprinting\"），§3.1 Task Formulation（d ∈ {0, 1, 2+}），Appendix d（5-class F1 ≈ 0.50）\n- 问题：\"Fingerprinting\" 在学术语境中暗示精细的、唯一性的识别能力（如 browser fingerprinting、device fingerprinting），但本文实际提供的是一个 3-class 粗粒度分类器（human / gen-1 / gen-2+）。更关键的是，Appendix d 显示 5-class 分类 F1 仅约 0.50（接近 gen-2/gen-3 不可区分），意味着超过 depth 2 后系统无法区分递归深度。将一个只能区分\"浅/中/深\"三个 bin 的分类器称为\"fingerprinting\"，会误导读者对论文实际贡献的预期。\n- 为什么影响接收：标题是读者的第一印象，framing 过度会导致 reviewer 产生 \"overclaimed\" 的判断，反而损害论文本来扎实的 empirical 贡献。\n- 建议修改方向：考虑 \"Depth Profiling\" 或 \"Depth Classification\" 等更准确的表述，或在 Abstract 第一句就明确 \"coarse ordinal classification\" 的定位。\n\n---\n\n## Should-Fix 问题（影响评分但不一票否决）\n\n**W1** [warning] Entropy/NLL 术语混淆\n\n- 具体位置：§3.3 Feature Extraction（\"We use 'entropy' as shorthand for token-level NLL\"），Table 1，全文\n- 问题：信息论 entropy $H(X)$ 和给定参考模型下的 token-level NLL $-\\log p_\\theta(x_t | x_{<t})$ 是不同的量——前者是真实分布的性质，后者是特定模型对特定 token 序列的 cross-entropy。论文虽然在 §3.3 有一句 disclaimer，但之后全文一直用 \"entropy mean / std / max / min\" 指代 NLL 统计量，这在信息论严格的 CL 社区容易引起混淆。特别是 Discussion §5 讨论 DDR 的信息论含义时，如果读者将 \"entropy\" 理解为真实 entropy 而非 reference-model NLL，会得出不同的结论。\n- 建议：在全文中统一使用 \"reference-model NLL\" 或 \"cross-entropy\"，仅在需要简写时使用 \"NLL\" 而非 \"entropy\"。Table 1 的 \"Source\" 列已区分了 \"Ref. NLL\" 和 \"Surface\"，但 feature 名称本身（entropy_mean 等）造成混淆。\n\n**W2** [warning] Baseline 比较不够公平\n\n- 具体位置：§4.3 Comparison with Detection Baselines，Table 3\n- 问题：Table 3 将 7-feature ensemble（多特征分类器）与 Avg-LogProb、FastDetectGPT、Binoculars、LD-Score 作为**单特征预测器**进行比较。这些 detection 方法设计目标是 binary human-vs-machine 分类，将它们作为单一特征 repurpose 为 depth 分类本身就处于劣势。更公平的比较应该是：(1) 将多个 detection score 组合为 multi-feature baseline，或 (2) 与专门设计的 ordinal 分类方法比较。虽然 Table 3 下方确实有 \"4-det ensemble\" 行（F1=0.743），但主文叙事仍侧重于单特征 vs. 7-feature 的优势，给人 strawman 比较的印象。\n- 建议：重新组织 §4.3 叙事，以 4-det+surface ensemble 作为主要 baseline 对比点，或增加更多 competitive baselines（如 n-gram 特征、stylometric 特征组合）。\n\n**W3** [warning] Primary metric 选择偏离实际需求\n\n- 具体位置：§4.1（\"we adopt leak-free binary F1 (gen-1 vs. gen-2+) = 0.694 as the primary metric\"）\n- 问题：如果论文的 motivation 是检测训练数据中的递归生成文本，最重要的实际问题是区分 gen-0（human）vs. gen-1+（any LLM text）——即 binary detection。论文选择 gen-1 vs. gen-2+ 作为 primary metric 是为了突出 DDR 的区分能力，但这优化了论文叙事而非实际应用。3-class macro-F1 = 0.762 或 gen-0 vs. gen-1+ binary F1 会是更直接的实用指标。\n- 建议：至少并列报告 gen-0 vs. all-AI 的 binary F1 作为 complementary metric，以便读者评估实际应用场景下的性能。\n\n**W4** [warning] Abstract 信息过载\n\n- 具体位置：Abstract\n- 问题：Abstract 在不到 250 词中塞入了 8+ 个数值结果（Cohen's d=0.76–1.17、d≤0.10、0.6B–8B parameters、F1=0.694、3-class F1=0.905 等），导致核心信息被数字淹没。读者在没有上下文的情况下很难判断这些数字意味着什么。一个好的 abstract 应该传达 *what* (task) → *how* (method) → *so what* (finding) 的叙事弧线，而不是数据倾泻。\n- 建议：减少数字到 3-4 个关键指标，增加一句 \"so what\" 的 high-level takeaway。\n\n**W5** [warning] Concurrent work 比较过于表面\n\n- 具体位置：§2.2 第二段（Mohamed et al., 2024; Geng et al., 2026），Appendix z.6（Table 45）\n- 问题：Table 45 的 concurrent work 比较仅列出 chain type、feature space、analysis goal、generator scope 等元数据，缺乏**实质性结果对比**。Mohamed et al. (2024) 研究 model collapse 的统计分析，Geng et al. (2026) 研究 iterative generation 下的 diversity dynamics——这些工作的核心发现是什么？与本文的 DDR 发现一致还是矛盾？表面的元数据对比无法帮助读者理解这些并行工作之间的知识关系。\n- 建议：增加 1-2 段文字讨论这些 concurrent work 的核心 finding 及其与 DDR 的关系（一致/互补/矛盾）。\n\n**W6** [warning] Reference model 同族偏差未充分讨论\n\n- 具体位置：§3.2（\"A held-out reference model (Pythia-410M)\"），Appendix s（cross-family references）\n- 问题：主实验的 reference model Pythia-410M 与两个 generator（Pythia-1B, Pythia-2.8B）同属 Pythia 家族，且共享 GPT-NeoX architecture。虽然 Appendix s（Table 26）显示 cross-family reference 结果一致（drift direction is consistent），但 Table 2 中 Pythia generators 的 F1 明显最高（Pythia-1B: 0.795, Pythia-2.8B: 0.714 vs. Mistral-7B: 0.684, OLMo-2-1B: 0.706）。这是否部分归因于同族 reference model 优势？主文应更显著地讨论这一潜在 confound。\n- 建议：在 §3.3 或 §4.1 增加一段讨论，引用 Appendix s 结果来 address 这个问题，或在 main results 中直接报告 cross-family reference 的对比。\n\n---\n\n## 小问题（不影响接收）\n\n1. **Appendix 结构过于庞大**：35 页论文中有 25+ 个 appendix section，部分 appendix（如 DDR partition validation, TOST equivalence）对主文 claim 的支撑是关键的，应考虑将最重要的 2-3 个 appendix 结果 **summarize 进主文**，而非全部外放。目前主文的读者需要频繁跳转附录才能验证核心 claim。\n\n2. **Figure 1 caption 信息量不足**：Figure 1 是论文的 key figure，但 caption 只描述了\"top row drifting / bottom row stationary\"，没有指出读者应该注意的 pattern（如跨 generator 一致性、d 值的含义）。\n\n3. **§3.1 depth 定义与实际实验的映射**：§3.1 定义 d ∈ {0, 1, 2+}，但实际生成链到 depth 4。从 depth 0-4 到 3-class {0, 1, 2+} 的 collapse 策略应在 §3.1 而非 §3.2 末尾说明。\n\n4. **Table 2 脚注 3 不易发现**：Pythia generators 使用原始 6-generator subset 而非完整 8-generator set 的说明放在脚注中容易被忽略，应在 table caption 中明确。\n\n5. **Limitations 中缺少对 English-only 的讨论**：所有实验均使用英文 Wikipedia seed，但 Limitations 未提及语言泛化性问题。\n\n---\n\n## Questions for Authors\n\n1. 如果将 reference model 换成一个完全不相关的架构（如 RWKV 或 Mamba），DDR partition 是否仍然成立？Appendix s 仅测试了 Transformer 家族内的替换。\n\n2. gen-2+ class 内部的 depth 分布如何？如果 gen-4 样本极少，3-class F1 可能被 gen-2（占 gen-2+ 大多数）的性能主导。Table 4 显示了 per-generator 样本数但未区分 gen-2/3/4 在 gen-2+ 中的占比。\n\n3. Discussion §5 提到 DDR 可能反映 nucleus sampling 的结构性质。如果使用 greedy decoding（temperature=0），DDR 是否消失？Appendix z.4 有部分数据但仅覆盖 3 个 generator。\n\n4. 论文是否考虑过将 DDR 特征与现有 detection 方法（如 FastDetectGPT score）结合，看是否能提升 human-vs-machine 的 binary detection 性能？这会是一个更有说服力的 practical contribution。\n\n---\n\n## Missing References\n\n1. **Xu et al. (2024/2025)** — 关于 paraphrase attack 对 AI text detection 的影响，与本文的 text perturbation robustness analysis (Appendix i) 相关但未引用专门针对 paraphrase detection 的近期工作。\n\n2. **Clark et al. (2021)** — \"All That's Human Is Not Gold\" — 人类对 AI 文本的判别能力基线，与本文的 gen-0 vs. gen-1 区分有概念关联。\n\n3. 论文引用列表中有部分 2025-2026 年的 arXiv 预印本（如 Geng et al., 2026），建议核实这些引用是否已正式发表或更新了 venue 信息。\n\n---\n\n## 主要优势\n\n1. **新颖的 task formulation**：将 recursive generation depth 作为一个独立的分析维度，区别于传统的 binary detection 和 source attribution，概念上有创新。\n\n2. **DDR 分解的清晰性**：drifting（location shift）vs. near-stationary（shape preservation）的二分法是一个优雅的 empirical finding，有良好的可解释性。\n\n3. **实验的系统性和严谨性**：LOGO 协议、TOST 等效检验、bootstrap CI、Granger causality、k-means partition validation——统计方法论水准在 CL 社区 empirical 论文中属于上乘。\n\n4. **附录的深度**：25+ 个 appendix section 覆盖了几乎所有可能的 confound 和 sensitivity 分析，体现了对 robustness 的认真态度。\n\n5. **诚实的 Limitations**：明确承认 base-model-only scope、3-class 分辨率上限、instruction-tuning 衰减等问题，没有回避负面结果。\n",

  "project": "gen-depth-fingerprint"
}

Discussion §5 提到 DDR 可能反映 nucleus sampling 的结构性质。如果使用 greedy decoding（temperature=0），DDR 是否消失？Appendix z.4 有部分数据但仅覆盖 3 个 generator。\n\n4. 论文是否考虑过将 DDR 特征与现有 detection 方法（如 FastDetectGPT score）结合，看是否能提升 human-vs-machine 的 binary detection 性能？这会是一个更有说服力的 practical contribution。\n\n---\n\n## Missing References\n\n1. **Xu et al. (2024/2025)** — 关于 paraphrase attack 对 AI text detection 的影响，与本文的 text perturbation robustness analysis (Appendix i) 相关但未引用专门针对 paraphrase detection 的近期工作。\n\n2. **Clark et al. (2021)** — \"All That's Human Is Not Gold\" — 人类对 AI 文本的判别能力基线，与本文的 gen-0 vs. gen-1 区分有概念关联。\n\n3. 论文引用列表中有部分 2025-2026 年的 arXiv 预印本（如 Geng et al., 2026），建议核实这些引用是否已正式发表或更新了 venue 信息。\n\n---\n\n## 主要优势\n\n1. **新颖的 task formulation**：将 recursive generation depth 作为一个独立的分析维度，区别于传统的 binary detection 和 source attribution，概念上有创新。\n\n2. **DDR 分解的清晰性**：drifting（location shift）vs. near-stationary（shape preservation）的二分法是一个优雅的 empirical finding，有良好的可解释性。\n\n3. **实验的系统性和严谨性**：LOGO 协议、TOST 等效检验、bootstrap CI、Granger causality、k-means partition validation——统计方法论水准在 CL 社区 empirical 论文中属于上乘。\n\n4. **附录的深度**：25+ 个 appendix section 覆盖了几乎所有可能的 confound 和 sensitivity 分析，体现了对 robustness 的认真态度。\n\n5. **诚实的 Limitations**：明确承认 base-model-only scope、3-class 分辨率上限、instruction-tuning 衰减等问题，没有回避负面结果。\n",

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
## Step 2 — 交叉分享：请逐条回应 Reviewer 1 的审稿意见

以下是 Reviewer 1 的完整审稿原文。请逐条回应：

特别关注：R1 提出了你未涉及的跨域温度混淆问题（N1），以及 DDR partition 循环性问题（W5）——请重点评估这些问题的严重性。

---

## Reviewer 1 — Round 1 审稿报告

**Overall**: 3.5 (Borderline Conference) | Soundness 4 | Excitement 3

### Must-Fix 问题

**N1** [critical] 跨域评估温度混淆——无法隔离 domain effect
- Appendix j 三个额外 domain（News, Academic, Code）使用 t=1.0, top-p=1.0，超出论文自定义可靠范围（t ≤ 0.7）。Table 12 证明 t=0.9 时 F1 下降 9.3-26.0 pp。因此 Table 16 的 cross-domain F1（0.634-0.779）无法区分 domain 迁移困难还是高温信号退化。
- 建议：在 t=0.7 下重复 cross-domain 实验，或至少提供一个 domain 的 t=0.7 对比数据。

**N2** [critical] 5-class depth 分辨率急剧退化，削弱 "fingerprinting" framing
- 5-class F1 降至 ~0.501（Table 5），gen-2/gen-3/gen-4 几乎不可区分。3-class 更接近 "detection with one extra class" 而非 "fingerprinting"。
- 建议：更早更显著讨论此限制，考虑调整术语。

### Should-Fix 问题

**W1** [warning] Gemma-2-2B anomaly 暴露架构敏感性
- 加入 Gemma-2-2B 后 gen-0/gen-1 系统性混淆，说明 DDR 并非 architecture-agnostic。
- 建议：量化偏移，讨论 unknown-architecture 场景影响。

**W2** [warning] Reference model（Pythia-410M）的 architecture bias
- 与两个 Pythia generator 共享 GPT-NeoX 架构。Cross-family 测试仅在 5-gen subset，且所有 reference 都是 causal LM。
- 建议：讨论 reference model architecture 理论约束。

**W3** [warning] 部分 appendix 实验仅在 6-generator subset
- LOFO、混淆矩阵、classifier sensitivity 用 6-gen subset，主文用 8-gen。LOFO 特征重要性排序可能不同。
- 建议：至少 LOFO 补充 8-gen 结果。

**W4** [warning] Downstream utility 实验 power 不足
- n=3 seeds 声称 depth-aware curation 等价于 random subsampling。正文引用时语气比 appendix 更肯定。
- 建议：正文加 qualifier 或增加 seeds。

**W5** [warning] DDR partition 定义的潜在循环性
- Partition 基于特征与 depth 的 Spearman ρ 定义，这些特征随后用于分类 depth。k-means 和 holdout 提供独立验证，但 partition 定义包含 target variable 信息。
- 建议：讨论 DDR 的 descriptive vs. mechanistic nature。

**W6** [warning] Instruction-tuned 模型下 DDR 大幅压缩
- 3-class F1 下降 3.7-19.9 pp；6/7 特征 ρ 反转。QLoRA 在 instruct 数据上仍有提升。Limitations 篇幅不足，未讨论 "unknown model type" 场景。
- 建议：Limitations 增加 unknown model type 讨论。

### 小问题
- I1: Table 1 特征名格式不统一
- I2: Figure 3 灰度可读性不足
- I3: 未提供 Anonymous GitHub link

### Questions
1. 能否提供 t=0.7 下非 Wikipedia domain 结果？
2. QLoRA features 能否替换 handcrafted features 恢复 instruct 模型下的 DDR？
3. 更大 reference model 对 DDR partition 的影响？
4. LOFO 为何未在 8-gen set 更新？

### Missing References
总体引用覆盖度良好，未发现明显遗漏。

### 主要优势
1. DDR 分解是 genuine contribution
2. 统计框架异常完善
3. LOGO 评估设计消除 generator leakage
4. 极其充分的消融实验
5. Honest scope delineation

1	Figure~\ref{fig:cross-temp} shows cross-temperature transfer results for the 6-generator LOGO pipeline.
2	We evaluate four train$\rightarrow$test temperature conditions using Qwen3-1.7B data regenerated at $t{=}1.0$ (remaining five generators retain $t{=}0.7$).\footnote{The $t{=}1.0$ data uses top-$p{=}0.95$ (vs.\ $p{=}0.9$ for other configurations); this difference may contribute to the observed degradation beyond the temperature effect alone.}
3	Key observations:
4	
5	\begin{enumerate}[nosep]
6	\item \textbf{Matched-temperature robustness}: Both $t{=}0.7$ and $t{=}1.0$ matched conditions yield comparable performance (3-class F1: 0.789 vs.\ 0.726), with the gap attributable to weaker depth signal at high temperature rather than classifier degradation.
7	
8	\item \textbf{Asymmetric transfer}: Training at $t{=}1.0$ and testing at $t{=}0.7$ (F1\,=\,0.791) transfers well, but the reverse (0.7$\rightarrow$1.0, F1\,=\,0.723) degrades. This asymmetry reflects the information structure: features learned from high-variance (high-temperature) data generalize to low-variance settings, but not vice versa.
9	
10	\item \textbf{Generator-specific vulnerability}: Qwen3-1.7B, the only generator with temperature-mismatched test data, drops to F1\,=\,0.418 under cross-temperature conditions while all other generators maintain F1 $>$ 0.76. This confirms that depth features are calibrated to the specific decoding regime.
11	
12	\item \textbf{Binary transfer for the mismatched generator}: For Qwen3-1.7B (the only generator with actual temperature mismatch), binary F1 drops from 0.948 to 0.713 under 0.7$\rightarrow$1.0 transfer but recovers in the reverse direction (1.0$\rightarrow$0.7: 0.948), consistent with the asymmetric pattern in Item 2. The aggregate binary range (0.900--0.958) is inflated by five generators whose data is identical across conditions.
13	\end{enumerate}

1	Can depth-aware data curation improve downstream language model quality?
2	We fine-tune OLMo-2-0425-1B on five data variants (3 seeds each) and evaluate perplexity on a held-out test set (Table~\ref{tab:downstream}).
3	
4	\begin{table}[ht]
5	\centering
6	\small
7	\caption{Downstream fine-tuning perplexity (OLMo-2-0425-1B, mean $\pm$ std across 3 seeds). At equal sample count, random subsampling (2.367) outperforms depth-aware curation (depth 0--1: 2.387, binary-filtered: 2.389).}
8	\label{tab:downstream}
9	\begin{tabular}{lrc}
10	\toprule
11	\textbf{Variant} & \textbf{$n$} & \textbf{Test PPL} \\
12	\midrule
13	All depths            & 12{,}299 & 2.335 $\pm$ 0.000 \\
14	Size-matched (random) & 3{,}951  & 2.367 $\pm$ 0.000 \\
15	Depth 0--1 only       & 3{,}951  & 2.387 $\pm$ 0.001 \\
16	Binary-filtered       & 3{,}951  & 2.389 $\pm$ 0.000 \\
17	Depth 0 only          & 797      & 2.681 $\pm$ 0.001 \\
18	\bottomrule
19	\end{tabular}
20	\end{table}
21	
22	The results are unambiguous: data quantity dominates depth composition.
23	Training on all 12{,}299 samples yields the lowest perplexity (2.335).
24	At equal sample count ($n$\,=\,3{,}951), random subsampling (2.367) \emph{outperforms} both depth-stratified selection (depth 0--1: 2.387) and binary detection filtering (2.389).
25	Cross-seed variance is below 0.002 across all variants, which confirms the robustness of this null result.
26	
27	Restricting training to human-only text (depth 0, $n$\,=\,797) produces a sharp perplexity increase to 2.681, driven primarily by the 5$\times$ reduction in data volume rather than depth composition per se.
28	
29	\paragraph{Equivalence testing.}
30	We apply a two one-sided $t$-test (TOST) with a $\pm$2\% relative margin (equivalence bounds $\pm$0.047 on the ppl scale) to formally assess whether the depth-aware and random subsampling conditions produce practically indistinguishable perplexity.

25	At $k{=}4$ (silhouette\,=\,0.571), entropy std separates from the near-stationary group, consistent with its intermediate DDR classification.

1	\section{Discussion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	%% Mechanism — why DDR exists
5	We hypothesize that the differential depth response reflects a structural property of nucleus sampling: top-$p$ truncation preserves the conditional distribution's functional form while recursive inputs grow increasingly self-typical, compressing expected NLL monotonically (Appendix~\ref{app:nll-distribution}); a formal derivation remains future work.
6	Two testable predictions follow---increasing randomness should weaken location drift, semantic constraints should attenuate it---both confirmed: at $\tau{=}1.0$, depth--NLL correlation collapses ($\rho{=}{-}0.002$; Appendix~\ref{app:sampling-sensitivity}); elaboration prompts reduce F1 to 0.730 (Appendix~\ref{app:prompt-type}). Drifting features contribute $5.5\times$ more (Appendix~\ref{app:ddr-causality}).
7	Shape stability holds even when drift is suppressed: at $\tau{=}1.0$ ($d < 0.15$; Appendix~\ref{app:ddr-temp-stability}), under greedy decoding (mean $|d|{=}0.05$), and with top-$k$ ($k{=}50$; Appendix~\ref{app:decoding-strategy}).
8	
9	%% Implications — what DDR enables
10	Depth constitutes a new operational dimension for corpus management that binary human-vs-machine classifiers cannot access.
11	The classifier supports batch contamination estimation (${\pm}3$--$4$\% at $n{=}1{,}000$; gen-1 per-class F1\,=\,0.63; Appendix~\ref{app:error-analysis}) rather than per-document filtering; depth-stratified curation can also serve as a prophylactic against model collapse \cite{drayson_detection_prevents_collapse}, though no downstream improvement over unfiltered baselines is detected (TOST $\varepsilon{=}{\pm}2\%$; limited power with $n{=}3$ seeds; Appendix~\ref{app:downstream}).
12	
13	%% Scope \& Connections — where DDR works and fails
14	DDR is mechanistically distinct from model collapse \cite{curse_of_recursion,strong_model_collapse}: retraining erodes tail coverage by updating weights, whereas inference-chain drift at fixed par

We characterize this as a preliminary observation rather than a definitive null result.\"\n\n这段 disclaimer 已经相当谨慎。如果正文确实引用时语气更强（我需要确认），那 R1 的建议（在正文加 qualifier）合理，但严重性不应超过 info 级别——论文在 appendix 中已经承认了局限。\n\n**严重性评估：维持 warning 但偏向 info。** 核心信息（preliminary null result with n=3）是诚实的。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE with nuance] R1-W5: DDR partition 循环性**\n\n这是 R1 提出的一个我未涉及的有趣方法论问题。让我仔细评估。\n\nR1 的论点：DDR partition 基于特征与 depth 的 Spearman ρ 定义（|ρ| ≥ ε 为 drifting），然后这些特征用于分类 depth。这存在潜在循环性——partition 定义中包含了 target variable (depth) 的信息。\n\n**我部分同意此担忧，但认为 Appendix v 的验证已经实质性地化解了循环风险：**\n\n1. **k-means 在 (|ρ|, d) 空间中聚类**：虽然 |ρ| 本身涉及 depth label，但 k-means 的结果表明 drifting/non-drifting 分裂是 feature 空间中最强的自然聚类结构（silhouette=0.660，远超第二名 0.18 gap）。如果 partition 只是 depth-label 的 artifact，不应该产生如此清晰的聚类。\n\n2. **Holdout generator split**：两个互补的 4/4 generator split 恢复了完全相同的 partition，说明这不是某个 generator 的特殊性质。\n\n3. **Exhaustive partition enumeration**：全部 35 种可能的 3/4 分裂中，观察到的 DDR partition 获得最高 silhouette，且 20/35 partition 产生负 silhouette。\n\n但 R1 说得对：**论文应更显式地讨论 DDR 的 descriptive vs. mechanistic nature**。当前 Discussion §5 的叙事暗示 DDR 反映了 nucleus sampling 的结构性质（mechanistic claim），但 partition 定义本身是 descriptive 的。这两个层次应更清晰地区分。DDR partition 是一个 well-supported descriptive observation，nucleus sampling hypothesis 是一个 testable but unproven mechanistic explanation。\n\n**严重性评估：同意 warning 级别。** [Round 1 审稿请求]:
你是 Reviewer 2，负责 **新颖性 + 完整性 + 写作质量** 的审查。

## 投稿信息
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (via ARR May 2026 cycle)
- **Deadline**: 2026-05-25
- **格式**: Long paper, ACL 模板, 正文 ≤8 页, double-blind
- **Limitations/Ethics**: 不计页数
- **参考文献**: 不限页数

## 论文路径
- LaTeX: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_3/projects/gen-depth-fingerprint/docs/paper/main.tex`
- PDF: `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_3/projects/gen-depth-fingerprint/artifacts/review_round_44/submitted.pdf`

## 论文概述
"Depth Profiling: Classifying Recursive Generation Depth in Base-Model LLM Text" — 提出 DDR（differential depth response）分解框架，将递归 LLM 生成的分布漂移分解为 location 和 shape 两部分，发现 NLL location 特征随 depth 单调漂移而 shape 特征近似不变，由此构建 3-class depth classifier（gen-0/gen-1/gen-2+）。实验覆盖 8 个 base-model generators（5 个 family），二分类 F1=0.694（leave-one-generator-out），端到端 QLoRA 3-class F1=0.905。

## 你的审查重点
1. **新颖性评估**：DDR 作为 empirical contribution 的 novelty 是否足够 EMNLP？与 concurrent work（Geng et al. 2026 Broken Telephone, Mohamed et al. 2025 Markovian Chains）的区分是否清晰？
2. **Claim-Evidence 匹配**：Abstract/Intro 的 claim scope 是否与实验结果匹配？"depth profiling" 与 3-class 实际分辨率的 framing gap？"approximately preserved" 是否 overselling？
3. **Scope 与 motivation**：base-model-only scope 是否在 Abstract/Intro 充分前置？Intro 的 real-world motivation（scientific publications, data provenance）与 base-model 实验的匹配度？
4. **Related Work 完整性**：是否有重要遗漏引用？concurrent work comparison table 是否实质性？
5. **写作质量**：术语一致性（entropy vs NLL）？Abstract 信息密度是否过高？叙事逻辑是否清晰？
6. **Baseline 比较**：叙事重心是否恰当（单特征 vs multi-feature ensemble）？是否给人 strawman 印象？
7. **Limitations 完整性**：English-only、scale ≤8B、instruction-tuned 失效等是否充分讨论？

请提供完整的结构化审稿报告：Summary → Strengths → Weaknesses (按 critical/major/minor 分级) → Questions → Missing References → Overall Assessment（评分 + 置信度）。

In case of any emergencies, the chairs should be warned via the emergency declaration form.

## Author Registration
After submission, all authors must complete the author registration form by May 27, 2026 EoD AoE.

_source line 4458 · 2026-05-22T07:23:24.481506+00:00_

# ARR Reviewer Scoring Dimensions

Based on the guidelines provided, ARR reviewers use **three scoring dimensions** (as of February 2025):

## The Three Scores

1. **Soundness**: Assesses technical quality and validity. The guidelines state that "soundness scores must be justified by the text of the review." Low soundness ratings require explicit justification in your written feedback.

2. **Excitement**: Reflects personal interest independent of soundness. This captures subjective preferences and "is orthogonal to the assessment of the soundness of the paper."

3. **Overall Assessment**: An explicit recommendation for acceptance/rejection to an ACL venue. This "composite score reflecting your assessment of soundness, excitement, and also other factors like novelty and impact."

## Key Guidance

The document emphasizes that soundness and excitement serve different purposes—you can find work sound but uninteresting to you personally, or vice versa. All papers recommended for publication must meet soundness standards, though personal excitement varies by reviewer.

However, **the specific numerical scales and detailed rubrics for each dimension are not explicitly detailed in this document**. The guidelines focus more on how to justify these scores through quality review writing rather than providing granular scoring definitions.

_source line 4459 · 2026-05-22T07:23:25.888743+00:00_

# ACL Rolling Review Form: Scoring Dimensions

## Reviewer Confidence
Scale: 1-5
- **5**: "Positive that my evaluation is correct. I read the paper very carefully and am familiar with related work."
- **4**: High confidence with unlikely but possible errors
- **3**: Generally confident but may have missed details
- **2**: Willing to defend evaluation but likely missed some points
- **1**: Outside expertise area or paper very difficult to understand

## Soundness
Scale: 1-5 (with half-point increments)
- **5**: "Excellent: This study is one of the most thorough I have seen, given its type."
- **4**: Strong support for claims; minor extra experiments optional
- **3**: Main claims sufficiently supported; minor points need details
- **2**: Main claims lack support; major technical/methodological problems
- **1**: "Major Issues: This study is not yet sufficiently thorough to warrant publication or is not relevant to ACL."

## Excitement
Scale: 1-5 (with half-point increments)
- **5**: "Highly Exciting: I would recommend this paper to others and/or attend its presentation."
- **4**: Would mention to others or attend presentation
- **3**: Might mention points or attend if time available
- **2**: Doesn't resonate personally but may appeal to community
- **1**: Won't resonate with broader computational linguistics community

## Overall Assessment
Scale: 1-5 (with half-point increments)
- **5**: Consider for Award (top 2.5% papers)
- **4**: Conference acceptance
- **3**: Accept to Findings track
- **2**: Resubmit after next cycle with substantial revisions
- **1**: Do not resubmit or not relevant to ACL

## Additional Dimensions

**Reproducibility** (1-5): Ranges from easily reproducible to impossible to reproduce

**Datasets** (1-5): From enabling impact to no usable datasets

**Software** (1-5): From enabling impact to no usable software

I've found the official ACL Rolling Review scoring rubrics and dimensions for 2025-2026. Here are the exact specifications:

### **Scoring Dimensions and Scales**

The ARR review form uses the following dimensions:

1. **Soundness** (1-5 scale with half-point increments)
   - 5: "Excellent: This study is one of the most thorough I have seen, given its type."
   - 4: Strong support for claims; minor extra experiments optional
   - 3: Main claims sufficiently supported; minor points need details
   - 2: Main claims lack support; major technical/methodological problems
   - 1: "Major Issues: This study is not yet sufficiently thorough to warrant publication or is not relevant to ACL."

   - 4: Would mention to others or attend presentation

   - 2: Doesn't resonate personally but may appeal to community
   - 1: Won't resonate with broader computational linguistics community

3. **Overall Assessment** (1-5 scale with half-point increments)
   - 5: Consider for Award (top 2.5% papers)
   - 4: Conference acceptance
   - 3: Accept to Findings track
   - 2: Resubmit after next cycle with substantial revisions
   - 1: Do not resubmit or not relevant to ACL

### **Additional Dimensions**

The form also includes optional scoring for:
- **Reproducibility** (1-5): From easily reproducible to impossible to reproduce

{
  "text": "## Reviewer 2 审稿报告（Round 1）：新颖性 + 完整性 + 写作质量\n\n### 总体评分\n**Overall**: 3.5 — Borderline Conference\n**一句话总结**: DDR 分解框架是有用的分析工具，但核心发现（递归生成压缩分布）与 model collapse 文献高度重叠，且 framing 的雄心（depth profiling for provenance）显著超出实际能力上限（3-class, base-model only, English Wikipedia）。\n\n### 官方维度打分（ACL ARR Review Form）\n\n| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |\n|------|------|----------|\n| Soundness | 3.5/5 | 方法论整体扎实（LOGO 交叉验证、seed sharing 控制、长度混淆分析），但 Abstract 中多处 claim 超出实验证据范围 |\n| Excitement | 3/5 | DDR 分解是有意义的分析贡献，但核心洞察在 model collapse 文献中已有基础，实际效用受限于 3-class 分辨率和 base-model 限制 |\n| Reproducibility | 4/5 | 方法描述详尽（超参数、evaluation protocol、feature extraction），附录提供充分的实验细节，尽管代码未公开 |\n| Confidence | 4/5 | 对 AI 文本检测和 model collapse 文献较为熟悉，逐页精读了全文含全部附录 |\n\n---\n\n### Must-Fix 问题（影响接收决定）\n\n**N1** [critical] Abstract 中 shape feature 的 \"near-stationary (d < 0.10)\" claim 与实际证据不完全匹配\n- 具体位置：Abstract 第5句 \"while shape features remain near-stationary (d < 0.10)\"；Section 4.2（page 6）；Table 1（page 3）\n- 问题：Abstract 声称所有 shape features 的 Cohen's d < 0.10，但 Section 4.2 明确将 entropy_std 归类为 \"intermediate\"（介于 drifting 和 near-stationary 之间）。Appendix v（page 29）的 k-means 验证也显示 entropy_std 独立形成一个 cluster，不属于 near-stationary 组。Abstract 的二分法（location vs. shape）掩盖了这个三分结构（drifting / intermediate / near-stationary），对读者形成误导。\n- 为什么影响接收：核心理论贡献（DDR 分解的 clean asymmetry）的精确性受损。Reviewer 会质疑作者是否在 cherry-pick 有利的 partition。\n- 建议修改方向：Abstract 中将 \"(d < 0.10)\" 改为更准确的表述，如 \"three near-stationary features (d < 0.10) and one intermediate feature\"，或在正文中更突出地讨论 entropy_std 的特殊地位。\n\n**N2** [critical] \"Depth profiling\" 标题与实际 3-class 分辨率之间的 framing gap\n- 具体位置：Title；Abstract 全文；Section 5（page 8）\"depth resolution is bounded at three classes\"\n- 问题：\"Depth Profiling\" 暗示能够精细地刻画生成深度，但实验表明系统仅能区分 3 个类别（gen-0 / gen-1 / gen-2+），且 5-class LOGO evaluation（Appendix d, Table 5）macro F1 降至约 0.50，相邻深度（2-4）几乎不可区分。虽然 Section 5 承认了这个上限，但标题和 Abstract 的 framing 远超实际能力。此外，Abstract 同时列出 binary F1 = 0.694（LOGO）和 QLoRA 3-class F1 = 0.905，但这两个数字来自截然不同的评估协议（LOGO vs. within-model 5-fold CV），未明确区分可能误导读者。\n- 为什么影响接收：Claim scope 超出实验证据是 ARR 审稿的核心扣分点。一个只能做 3-class 分类的系统标题叫 \"Depth Profiling\" 属于 overclaiming。\n- 建议修改方向：（1）标题考虑改为更精确的表述如 \"Classifying Shallow vs. Deep Recursive Generation\"；（2）Abstract 在提及两个 F1 数字时明确注明评估协议差异；（3）在 Abstract 中前置 3-class 上限的 qualification。\n\n**N3** [critical] DDR 7-feature ensemble 与 detection baseline ensemble 性能持平，削弱 unique contribution 论述\n- 具体位置：Section 4.3（page 7-8）\"The 7-feature ensemble matches the 4-detector+surface ensemble (binary F1: 0.705 vs. 0.708)\"；Table 3（page 8）；Figure 3（page 8）\n- 问题：论文的核心 contribution 之一是 DDR 特征集作为 depth classifier 的有效性。但 Section 4.3 显示，7-feature DDR ensemble 的 binary F1（0.705）与 4-detector+surface ensemble（0.708）几乎完全一致。论文以 \"2.4× lower cost\" 作为优势论据，但 inference cost 并非本文的核心 claim。这意味着 DDR 分解的分类价值（classification utility）并未超越已有检测方法的简单组合——DDR 的贡献主要在分析层面（interpretability），而非性能层面。\n- 为什么影响接收：如果 DDR 特征集不比现有方法强，且 DDR 的贡献仅在分析/解释层面，那 empirical contribution 的 bar 可能不够 EMNLP main。论文需要更诚实地定位自己的贡献性质。\n- 建议修改方向：（1）在 Contributions 部分明确区分 \"分析贡献\"（DDR 分解洞察）和 \"工程贡献\"（分类器性能），承认后者并非核心 claim；（2）强化 DDR 分解在理解递归生成机制方面的独特价值，而非暗示性能优势。\n\n---\n\n### Should-Fix 问题（影响评分但不一票否决）\n\n**W1** [warning] 与 model collapse 文献的 novelty 区分不够清晰\n- 具体位置：Section 2.2（page 2-3）；Appendix z.6（Table 46, page 36）\n- 问题：递归生成导致文本分布压缩（NLL 下降、词汇多样性降低）是 model collapse 文献（Shumailov et al. 2024; Dohmatob et al. 2024, 2025）的已知结论。DDR 的新贡献是将这个压缩分解为 location shift 和 shape invariance 两个组分，并构建 ordinal classifier。但正文中对这个增量贡献的定位不够清晰——Related Work（Section 2.2）主要叙述 concurrent work 做了什么，较少突出 \"DDR 相比这些工作多解决了什么问题\"。Appendix z.6 的比较表（Table 46）提供了很好的结构化对比，但放在附录太深，正文中仅一句话提及。\n- 建议：将 Table 46 的核心区分点上提到 Related Work 正文中，用 1-2 句话明确 DDR 的 delta。\n\n**W2** [warning] Base-model-only scope 与 real-world motivation 的错位\n- 具体位置：Section 1（page 1）\"scientific publications, data provenance, principled corpus curation\"；Section 7 Limitations（page 8-9）；Appendix k（page 20-21）\n- 问题：Introduction 用 scientific publications 和 data provenance 作为 motivation，但这些 real-world 场景通常涉及 instruction-tuned models（如 ChatGPT 生成的论文）。论文的全部主实验仅覆盖 base models ≤8B。Appendix k 显示 instruction tuning 将 3-class F1 降低 3.7-19.9 pp，DDR structure 在 instruction-tuned 模型下被 \"压缩\"（Table 18-19）。这意味着论文的 motivation 与其实际能力之间存在系统性 gap。\n- 建议：在 Abstract 或 Introduction 第1段明确限定 scope（\"we study base-model recursive generation\"），避免用 instruction-tuned 场景作为隐含动机。\n\n**W3** [warning] Downstream utility 实验结果反向削弱 motivation\n- 具体位置：Appendix m（page 22-23）；Table 22-23\n- 问题：论文在 Introduction 中以 \"principled corpus curation\" 为 motivation 之一，但 Appendix m 的 downstream fine-tuning 实验明确表明 \"data quantity dominates depth composition\"——深度感知策划在等量采样下与随机采样统计等价（TOST 等价性检验通过）。这个结果实际上否定了 depth profiling 在数据策划场景中的实际效用，与 Introduction 的动机叙述矛盾。\n- 建议：在 Discussion 或 Limitations 中诚实讨论这个 negative result，调整 Introduction 中对 corpus curation 动机的措辞，或提供更有说服力的 downstream task 评估。\n\n**W4** [warning] 术语不一致：\"entropy\" vs. NLL\n- 具体位置：Section 3.3（page 4）\"we refer to per token cross-entropy loss under a fixed reference model as 'entropy'\"；Table 1（page 3）所有以 \"entropy_\" 开头的特征名\n- 问题：Per-token cross-entropy loss under a reference model 是 cross-entropy H(p, q)，不是 entropy H(p)。虽然 Section 3.3 做了注释说明这是 \"conciseness\"，且检测文献中这种用法有先例，但这仍然是技术上的 imprecision。7 个特征中 5 个以 \"entropy_\" 命名，但实际计算的是 reference-model NLL。这可能让来自信息论背景的 reviewer 困惑。\n- 建议：考虑将特征名统一为 \"nll_mean\", \"nll_std\" 等，或至少在 Table 1 中注明 \"computed as reference-model NLL\"。\n\n**W5** [warning] Abstract 信息密度过高\n- 具体位置：Abstract（page 1）\n- 问题：Abstract 在约 150 词内打包了 DDR 框架定义、location/shape 分解、具体 d 值范围（0.76-1.17, < 0.10）、两种评估协议的 F1（0.694 和 0.905）、QLoRA 方法等。信息密度极高，首次阅读难以消化。关键的 qualifier（如 3-class 上限、base-model only）被压缩或省略。\n- 建议：精简数字细节（如移除 d 值范围到正文），腾出空间前置关键 scope 限定。\n\n---\n\n### 小问题（不影响接收）\n\n- **Gemma-2-2B 异常**（Appendix z.5, page 35）：9-generator 扩展评估中 Gemma-2-2B 的 3-class F1 = 0.028（近似随机），虽然不在主实验 8-generator set 中，但作为唯一完全失败的模型家族，对泛化声明（\"across five families\"）构成隐忧。建议在 Limitations 中提及。\n- **Figure 1**（page 6）：strip plot 信息密度高但不同 generator 的颜色区分在灰度打印下可能难以辨认，考虑增加 marker 形状区分。\n- **Appendix 标号系统**：附录从 a 到 z.7，标号系统庞大（26+ sections），导航困难。考虑合并相关小节。\n\n---\n\n### 主要优势\n\n1. **DDR 分解框架**概念清晰、可操作——将 7 个特征分为 drifting / near-stationary / intermediate，为理解递归生成提供了结构化的分析视角（Section 4.2, Figure 1-2）。\n2. **实验设计严谨**：LOGO 交叉验证避免 generator leakage、seed sharing 控制消除 train-test overlap、长度匹配子采样排除长度混淆（Appendix b）、多种 robustness check（perturbation: Appendix i; prompt type: Appendix c; temperature: Appendix h; cross-domain: Appendix j）。\n3. **附录极其充分**（25页）：几乎所有可预见的 reviewer 问题都有对应分析——DDR partition validation（Appendix v）、TOST equivalence testing（Appendix u）、Granger causality（Appendix z.3）、cross-generator transfer matrix（Appendix y, Table 39）、decoding strategy robustness（Appendix z.4）等。\n4. **Concurrent work 比较**（Appendix z.6, Table 46）提供了结构化对比框架，明确了本文与 Mohamed et al. (2025) 和 Geng et al. (2026) 的互补关系。\n5. **9-generator 扩展**（Appendix z.5）和 **cross-domain 泛化**（Appendix j）提供了额外的泛化证据。\n\n---\n\n### 提问（Questions for Authors）\n\n1. 为什么选择 Pythia-410M 作为唯一 reference model？Appendix s 显示 cross-family reference models 有可比性能，但如果 reference model 的选择对 DDR partition 结构本身有影响（不仅仅是分类性能），这需要更多讨论。\n2. DDR 分解在 instruction-tuned 模型下 \"被压缩\" 的具体机制是什么？是 RLHF 的 reward hacking 改变了 distributional dynamics，还是 instruction-tuned 模型的 decoding diversity 根本不同？\n3. 如果将 7-feature DDR set 与 4-detector scores 合并为 11-feature ensemble，是否能突破当前 3-class 性能上限？这能帮助区分 DDR 是否提供了与 detection scores 正交的信息。\n\n---\n\n### 缺失引用\n\n未发现重大遗漏。引用列表覆盖了主要检测方法（Mitchell et al. 2023; Bao et al. 2024; Hans et al. 2024）、model collapse 核心文献（Shumailov et al. 2024; Dohmatob et al. 2024, 2025）、concurrent work（Geng et al. 2026; Mohamed et al. 2025）、以及相关 stylometry/provenance 工作。引用数量充分（70+），无明显遗漏。",
  "reviewer_id": "2",
  "project": "gen-depth-fingerprint"
}

同时列出 binary F1 = 0.694（LOGO）和 QLoRA 3-class F1 = 0.905，但这两个数字来自截然不同的评估协议（LOGO vs. within-model 5-fold CV），未明确区分可能误导读者。\n- 为什么影响接收：Claim scope 超出实验证据是 ARR 审稿的核心扣分点。一个只能做 3-class 分类的系统标题叫 \"Depth Profiling\" 属于 overclaiming。\n- 建议修改方向：（1）标题考虑改为更精确的表述如 \"Classifying Shallow vs. Deep Recursive Generation\"；（2）Abstract 在提及两个 F1 数字时明确注明评估协议差异；（3）在 Abstract 中前置 3-class 上限的 qualification。\n\n**N3** [critical] DDR 7-feature ensemble 与 detection baseline ensemble 性能持平，削弱 unique contribution 论述\n- 具体位置：Section 4.3（page 7-8）\"The 7-feature ensemble matches the 4-detector+surface ensemble (binary F1: 0.705 vs. 0.708)\"；Table 3（page 8）；Figure 3（page 8）\n- 问题：论文的核心 contribution 之一是 DDR 特征集作为 depth classifier 的有效性。但 Section 4.3 显示，7-feature DDR ensemble 的 binary F1（0.705）与 4-detector+surface ensemble（0.708）几乎完全一致。论文以 \"2.4× lower cost\" 作为优势论据，但 inference cost 并非本文的核心 claim。这意味着 DDR 分解的分类价值（classification utility）并未超越已有检测方法的简单组合——DDR 的贡献主要在分析层面（interpretability），而非性能层面。\n- 为什么影响接收：如果 DDR 特征集不比现有方法强，且 DDR 的贡献仅在分析/解释层面，那 empirical contribution 的 bar 可能不够 EMNLP main。论文需要更诚实地定位自己的贡献性质。\n- 建议修改方向：（1）在 Contributions 部分明确区分 \"分析贡献\"（DDR 分解洞察）和 \"工程贡献\"（分类器性能），承认后者并非核心 claim；（2）强化 DDR 分解在理解递归生成机制方面的独特价值，而非暗示性能优势。\n\n---\n\n### Should-Fix 问题（影响评分但不一票否决）\n\n**W1** [warning] 与 model collapse 文献的 novelty 区分不够清晰\n- 具体位置：Section 2.2（page 2-3）；Appendix z.6（Table 46, page 36）\n- 问题：递归生成导致文本分布压缩（NLL 下降、词汇多样性降低）是 model collapse 文献（Shumailov et al. 2024; Dohmatob et al. 2024, 2025）的已知结论。DDR 的新贡献是将这个压缩分解为 location shift 和 shape invariance 两个组分，并构建 ordinal classifier。但正文中对这个增量贡献的定位不够清晰——Related Work（Section 2.2）主要叙述 concurrent work 做了什么，较少突出 \"DDR 相比这些工作多解决了什么问题\"。Appendix z.6 的比较表（Table 46）提供了很好的结构化对比，但放在附录太深，正文中仅一句话提及。\n- 建议：将 Table 46 的核心区分点上提到 Related Work 正文中，用 1-2 句话明确 DDR 的 delta。\n\n**W2** [warning] Base-model-only scope 与 real-world motivation 的错位\n- 具体位置：Section 1（page 1）\"scientific publications, data provenance, principled corpus curation\"；Section 7 Limitations（page 8-9）；Appendix k（page 20-21）\n- 问题：Introduction 用 scientific publications 和 data provenance 作为 motivation，但这些 real-world 场景通常涉及 instruction-tuned models（如 ChatGPT 生成的论文）。论文的全部主实验仅覆盖 base models ≤8B。Appendix k 显示 instruction tuning 将 3-class F1 降低 3.7-19.9 pp，DDR structure 在 instruction-tuned 模型下被 \"压缩\"（Table 18-19）。这意味着论文的 motivation 与其实际能力之间存在系统性 gap。\n- 建议：在 Abstract 或 Introduction 第1段明确限定 scope（\"we study base-model recursive generation\"），避免用 instruction-tuned 场景作为隐含动机。\n\n**W3** [warning] Downstream utility 实验结果反向削弱 motivation\n- 具体位置：Appendix m（page 22-23）；Table 22-23\n- 问题：论文在 Introduction 中以 \"principled corpus curation\" 为 motivation 之一，但 Appendix m 的 downstream fine-tuning 实验明确表明 \"data quantity dominates depth composition\"——深度感知策划在等量采样下与随机采样统计等价（TOST 等价性检验通过）。这个结果实际上否定了 depth profiling 在数据策划场景中的实际效用，与 Introduction 的动机叙述矛盾。\n- 建议：在 Discussion 或 Limitations 中诚实讨论这个 negative result，调整 Introduction 中对 corpus curation 动机的措辞，或提供更有说服力的 downstream task 评估。\n\n**W4** [warning] 术语不一致：\"entropy\" vs. NLL\n- 具体位置：Section 3.3（page 4）\"we refer to per token cross-entropy loss under a fixed reference model as 'entropy'\"；Table 1（page 3）所有以 \"entropy_\" 开头的特征名\n- 问题：Per-token cross-entropy loss under a reference model 是 cross-entropy H(p, q)，不是 entropy H(p)。虽然 Section 3.3 做了注释说明这是 \"conciseness\"，且检测文献中这种用法有先例，但这仍然是技术上的 imprecision。7 个特征中 5 个以 \"entropy_\" 命名，但实际计算的是 reference-model NLL。这可能让来自信息论背景的 reviewer 困惑。\n- 建议：考虑将特征名统一为 \"nll_mean\", \"nll_std\" 等，或至少在 Table 1 中注明 \"computed as reference-model NLL\"。\n\n**W5** [warning] Abstract 信息密度过高\n- 具体位置：Abstract（page 1）\n- 问题：Abstract 在约 150 词内打包了 DDR 框架定义、location/shape 分解、具体 d 值范围（0.76-1.17, < 0.10）、两种评估协议的 F1（0.694 和 0.905）、QLoRA 方法等。信息密度极高，首次阅读难以消化。关键的 qualifier（如 3-class 上限、base-model only）被压缩或省略。\n- 建议：精简数字细节（如移除 d 值范围到正文），腾出空间前置关键 scope 限定。\n\n---\n\n### 小问题（不影响接收）\n\n- **Gemma-2-2B 异常**（Appendix z.5, page 35）：9-generator 扩展评估中 Gemma-2-2B 的 3-class F1 = 0.028（近似随机），虽然不在主实验 8-generator set 中，但作为唯一完全失败的模型家族，对泛化声明（\"across five families\"）构成隐忧。建议在 Limitations 中提及。\n- **Figure 1**（page 6）：strip plot 信息密度高但不同 generator 的颜色区分在灰度打印下可能难以辨认，考虑增加 marker 形状区分。\n- **Appendix 标号系统**：附录从 a 到 z.7，标号系统庞大（26+ sections），导航困难。考虑合并相关小节。\n\n---\n\n### 主要优势\n\n1. **DDR 分解框架**概念清晰、可操作——将 7 个特征分为 drifting / near-stationary / intermediate，为理解递归生成提供了结构化的分析视角（Section 4.2, Figure 1-2）。\n2. **实验设计严谨**：LOGO 交叉验证避免 generator leakage、seed sharing 控制消除 train-test overlap、长度匹配子采样排除长度混淆（Appendix b）、多种 robustness check（perturbation: Appendix i; prompt type: Appendix c; temperature: Appendix h; cross-domain: Appendix j）。\n3. **附录极其充分**（25页）：几乎所有可预见的 reviewer 问题都有对应分析——DDR partition validation（Appendix v）、TOST equivalence testing（Appendix u）、Granger causality（Appendix z.3）、cross-generator transfer matrix（Appendix y, Table 39）、decoding strategy robustness（Appendix z.4）等。\n4. **Concurrent work 比较**（Appendix z.6, Table 46）提供了结构化对比框架，明确了本文与 Mohamed et al. (2025) 和 Geng et al. (2026) 的互补关系。\n5. **9-generator 扩展**（Appendix z.5）和 **cross-domain 泛化**（Appendix j）提供了额外的泛化证据。\n\n---\n\n### 提问（Questions for Authors）\n\n1. 为什么选择 Pythia-410M 作为唯一 reference model？Appendix s 显示 cross-family reference models 有可比性能，但如果 reference model 的选择对 DDR partition 结构本身有影响（不仅仅是分类性能），这需要更多讨论。\n2. DDR 分解在 instruction-tuned 模型下 \"被压缩\" 的具体机制是什么？是 RLHF 的 reward hacking 改变了 distributional dynamics，还是 instruction-tuned 模型的 decoding diversity 根本不同？\n3. 如果将 7-feature DDR set 与 4-detector scores 合并为 11-feature ensemble，是否能突破当前 3-class 性能上限？这能帮助区分 DDR 是否提供了与 detection scores 正交的信息。\n\n---\n\n### 缺失引用\n\n未发现重大遗漏。引用列表覆盖了主要检测方法（Mitchell et al. 2023; Bao et al. 2024; Hans et al. 2024）、model collapse 核心文献（Shumailov et al. 2024; Dohmatob et al. 2024, 2025）、concurrent work（Geng et al. 2026; Mohamed et al. 2025）、以及相关 stylometry/provenance 工作。引用数量充分（70+），无明显遗漏。",

以下是 Reviewer 1 的完整审稿意见，请逐条回应：对每个 Must-Fix 和 Should-Fix 问题标注 [AGREE] 同意并说明理由，或 [REBUT] 反驳并提供证据（cite 具体 Section/Table/Figure）。

# Reviewer 1 审稿报告：方法严谨性 + 实验设计

## 总体评分
**Overall**: 3.5 — Borderline Conference

## 官方维度打分

| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |
|------|------|----------|
| Soundness | 3.5/5 | DDR partition 通过多重验证（k-means、per-generator replication、exhaustive enumeration、TOST + bootstrap），统计方法整体规范；但理论动机仅为假设（§5 "a formal derivation remains future work"），boundary features 使 clean partition 叙事模糊 |
| Excitement | 3/5 | Location/shape 分解视角新颖，为 recursive generation 研究提供了结构化工具；但 binary F1 ≈ 0.70 的实用性有限，且 instruction-tuned model 场景退化显著（-3.7 到 -19.9 pp），制约了影响力 |
| Reproducibility | 4/5 | 实验设置详尽（附录 25+ 个分析），软件版本、计算资源明确（4× RTX 5090, 62 GPU-hours），特征定义清晰；但未提及代码/数据公开计划 |
| Confidence | 4/5 | 本人熟悉 AI text detection 和统计检验方法，仔细阅读了全文 37 页 |

## Must-Fix 问题

**N1** [critical] DDR 理论动机薄弱，核心机制停留在假设层面
- §5 Discussion 第1段；§4.2 第1段
- 对 *为什么* location features drift 而 shape features 保持 stationary 的解释仅为假设——"we hypothesize this reflects the mixing property of finite-vocabulary autoregressive processes"（§4.2），引用了 Markovian chains 文章但无形式化推导。§5 明确承认 "a formal derivation remains future work"。
- DDR 框架的价值取决于其是 principled decomposition 还是 post-hoc observation。缺乏理论支撑，读者无法判断该 partition 是否在新条件下仍然成立。
- 建议：至少需要简化模型下的 sketch proof 或明确论证为什么 entropy max/min 不受 input distribution 的 self-typicality 压缩影响。

**N2** [critical] Cross-model chain 的 F1 提升缺乏替代假设排除
- §4.1 "Cross-model generalization" 段
- Cross-model chains 的 binary F1 = 0.830 显著高于 single-generator LOGO (0.694)。存在未被讨论的替代假设：cross-model chain 中每步引入不同 generator 的 stylistic fingerprint，分类器可能在区分 *generator diversity accumulation* 而非 *depth per se*。
- 建议：控制 generator diversity（如同 family 不同 size），或分析 drifting vs stationary features 在 cross-model 中的贡献比例。

## Should-Fix 问题

**W1** [major] Boundary features 使 DDR partition 的 "clean binary split" 叙事过于简化
- §4.2；Appendix v
- tail_decay 仅 4/8 generators 通过 TOST，entropy_min 在 gen-0/gen-1 边界敏感（|ρ|=0.40），entropy_std 为 "sole intermediate"（d=0.55）。实际 partition 是 3/1/1/2 而非论文主张的 3/1/3。

**W2** [major] Abstract 中 3-class F1 的呈现可能误导读者
- Abstract "an end-to-end classifier reaches 3-class F1 = 0.905"
- QLoRA 3-class F1=0.905 未附带 seed sharing caveat 和评估协议差异说明。

**W3** [major] XGBoost vs QLoRA 11.6 pp gap 的解释缺乏实证
- §4.1 倒数第2段
- "The 11.6 pp gap suggests token embeddings capture sequential patterns beyond the seven summary statistics" 是纯推测，未区分输入表示、联合优化、模型容量等因素。

**W4** [minor-major] 6-gen vs 8-gen subset 不一致增加结果比较认知负担
- QLoRA 3-class F1 = 0.905（6-gen）vs Table 2 3-class F1 = 0.762（8-gen），差异中多少来自 QLoRA 本身、多少来自 subset 差异无法判断。

**W5** [minor] Pythia-410M reference model 的 family proximity concern 未被充分定量
- per-generator F1 从 Pythia-2.8B 的 0.724 到 Mistral-7B 的 0.633，差距 9.1 pp，但 family proximity 在其中的贡献比例未量化。

## 小问题
1. Table 3 caption 过长
2. Figure 3 y轴范围放大了方法间差异
3. Appendix 编号系统导航困难
4. "Entropy" 术语歧义
5. Downstream utility n=3 统计功效不足

entropy_std 是 d=0.55 的 sole intermediate\n\n实际 partition 结构如 R1 所述是 **3 drifting / 1 intermediate(entropy_std) / 1 boundary-sensitive(entropy_min) / 2 near-stationary(entropy_max, tail_decay，且 tail_decay 一致性存疑)**，远非 Abstract 声称的 clean \"location vs shape\" 二分法。\n\n这个问题的严重性在于：DDR 的核心理论贡献就是这个 clean asymmetry。如果 partition 实际上是 messy 的，那 DDR 从 \"principled decomposition\" 降格为 \"approximate empirical tendency\"，论文的 novelty 进一步削弱。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W2: Abstract 中 QLoRA 3-class F1 的呈现可能误导读者**\n\n同意，这与我的 N2 一致。Abstract 将 LOGO binary F1=0.694 和 QLoRA 3-class F1=0.905 并列，但两者的评估协议完全不同——LOGO 是 leave-one-generator-out（最严格的泛化测试），QLoRA 是 within-model 5-fold CV on 6-gen subset（允许同一 generator 出现在 train 和 test 中）。不了解细节的读者很容易将 0.905 理解为系统的 generalized performance。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W3: XGBoost vs QLoRA 11.6 pp gap 的解释缺乏实证**\n\n同意。\"token embeddings capture sequential patterns beyond the seven summary statistics\" 是一个合理的猜测，但在没有以下任何一种验证的情况下就写入论文属于 unsubstantiated claim：\n1. Ablation：QLoRA on same 7 features vs. QLoRA on raw tokens\n2. Attention analysis：QLoRA 是否关注 sequential pattern\n3. 至少一个 intermediate representation 的 feature importance 分析\n\nR1 正确指出了三个无法区分的因素：input representation（summary stats vs. token embeddings）、joint optimization（XGBoost vs. gradient-based）、model capacity（shallow tree vs. transformer）。任何一个都可能解释 11.6 pp gap。\n\n---\n\n**[AGREE] R1-W4: 6-gen vs 8-gen subset 不一致**\n\n同意这是 presentation 层面的问题。QLoRA evaluation（6-gen, Appendix e）与 main Table 2（8-gen LOGO）使用不同的 generator subset，但 Abstract 将两者的 F1 并列呈现，增加了读者的认知负担。至少应在 Abstract 或 Table 2 caption 中注明 subset 差异。\n\n---\n\n**[REBUT] R1-W5: Pythia-410M reference model 的 family proximity concern 未被充分定量**\n\n部分反驳。R1 指出 per-generator F1 从 Pythia-2.8B (0.724) 到 Mistral-7B (0.633) 的 9.1 pp 差距可能反映 family proximity bias。这是一个合理的 concern，但论文在 **Appendix s**（page 27）已提供了 cross-family reference model validation：\n\n- Table 27（Cross-family reference model validation）显示使用非 Pythia 家族的 reference models（OLMo-2-1B, Qwen3-0.6B）时，LOGO F1 的 spread 仅为 0.013，与 Pythia-410M 的结果可比。\n- Appendix s 的 independent-seed control（page 27）进一步排除了 topic leakage。\n\n这些分析已经 **部分** 定量化了 family proximity 的影响——不同 family 的 reference models 产生类似的 DDR partition 和分类性能，说明 family proximity 不是 per-generator F1 variance 的主要解释因素。\n\n但我同意 R1 的隐含观点：Appendix s 验证了 reference model 选择的 **robustness**，但未直接回答 \"9.1 pp variance 中有多少来自 family proximity vs. generator 本身的 distributional property\"。一个 regression analysis（将 family distance 作为 predictor）可以更精确地量化这个贡献。\n\n---\n\n### 小问题回应\n\n1. **Table 3 caption 过长** — [AGREE]，caption 包含了过多 configuration detail，可移至正文或 footnote。\n2. **Figure 3 y 轴范围** — [AGREE]，y 轴从约 0.4 起始确实放大了方法间差异的视觉效果，建议标注 broken axis 或从 0 起始。\n3. **Appendix 编号导航困难** — [AGREE]，我在自己的审稿中也提到了这个问题（26+ sections，从 a 到 z.7）。\n4. **\"Entropy\" 术语歧义** — [AGREE]，我在 W4 中详细讨论了这个问题。\n5. **Downstream utility n=3 统计功效不足** — [AGREE]，我在 W3 中从另一个角度（downstream result 反向削弱 motivation）也提到了这个实验的问题。n=3 seeds 下 TOST 等价性检验的 power 确实有限，这使得 \"data quantity dominates\" 的结论不够 robust。\n\n---\n\n### 综合评价\n\nR1 和 R2 的审稿意见高度 convergent，核心分歧很小：\n\n**共识问题**（两位 Reviewer 独立发现）：\n1. DDR partition 的 clean binary 叙事被 boundary features 打破（R1-W1 ≈ R2-N1）\n2. Abstract 中 QLoRA F1 的呈现缺乏 protocol 区分（R1-W2 ≈ R2-N2）\n3. 术语不一致（R1-小问题4 ≈ R2-W4）\n4. Appendix 导航困难（R1-小问题3 ≈ R2-小问题）\n\n**R1 独特贡献**（我遗漏或未深入的）：\n1. **R1-N2 cross-model chain confound** — 非常有价值的 alternative hypothesis，我在自己的审稿中未涉及\n2. **R1-W3 XGBoost vs QLoRA gap** — 我未单独标记这个 unsubstantiated claim\n3. **R1-W4 6-gen vs 8-gen subset** — 我在 N2 中提及但未单独列出\n\n**总体**：我维持 Overall 3.5（Borderline Conference）的评分。论文的实验设计和附录覆盖度值得肯定，但 DDR 的理论动机不足（R1-N1）、framing overclaiming（R2-N1/N2）、以及与现有方法性能持平（R2-N3）共同构成了一组 coherent 的 concerns，使其更适合 Findings 而非 Main Conference。",

[Round 1 审稿请求]:
你是 Reviewer 2，负责审查论文的新颖性、完整性、写作质量和格式规范。

## Venue 信息
- **目标会议**: EMNLP 2026（通过 ARR 审稿）
- **截止日期**: 2026-05-25
- **页数限制**: 正文 8 页（不含 references、limitations、ethics）；附录无限制但非必须审查
- **模板**: ACL template（acl.sty, [review] option）
- **匿名规则**: Double-blind

## 论文路径
- TeX 源码: `projects/gen-depth-fingerprint/docs/paper/main.tex`
- PDF: `projects/gen-depth-fingerprint/artifacts/review_round_45/submitted.pdf`

## 你的审查侧重

### 1. 新颖性评估
- "Depth Profiling"（生成深度分类）作为新任务定义的原创性如何？与现有 AI text detection 工作的区别是否清晰？
- DDR（Differential Depth Response）分解——location drift vs shape stability——的发现是否构成足够的学术贡献？
- 与并发工作（Mohamed et al. 2025、Geng et al. 2026、Wang et al. 2025）的关系处理是否得当？

### 2. Related Work 完整性
- AI text detection、model collapse、vocabulary diversity 三个领域的覆盖是否充分？
- 是否遗漏了重要的相关工作？特别注意 2025-2026 年的新进展。
- 与 ordinal classification 文献的联系是否充分？

### 3. 写作质量
- 论文的 narrative arc 是否清晰？从问题动机 → DDR 发现 → 分类应用的逻辑链是否连贯？
- Abstract 和 Introduction 是否准确概括了论文贡献？是否有过度宣称？
- 术语使用是否一致（特别是"entropy"的定义——论文明确说这是 NLL 不是 information-theoretic entropy）？
- Discussion 部分的 nucleus sampling 假说是否讨论充分？
- Limitations 是否覆盖了关键局限？

### 4. 图表质量
- Figure 1（strip plot）、Figure 2（DDR summary）、Figure 3（baseline comparison）的信息密度和可读性
- 表格格式是否符合 ACL 模板规范？
- 附录中图表的必要性和质量

### 5. 格式规范
- 正文是否严格在 8 页以内？
- 匿名化是否彻底（无作者信息泄露、无非匿名仓库链接）？
- 引用格式是否符合 ACL/natbib 规范？
- Limitations 和 Ethics 部分是否符合 EMNLP 2026 要求？

### 6. 论文定位
- 这篇论文是 empirical study——贡献的深度和广度是否达到 EMNLP main conference 的门槛？
- 如果不够 main，是否适合 Findings？

请给出完整的审稿意见，包含 Summary、Strengths、Weaknesses、Questions、Missing References 各部分。对每个 weakness 标注严重等级：[Critical]、[Major]、[Minor]。

Based on the search results, I found information about the ACL Rolling Review (ARR) 2025/2026 reviewer guidelines:

## Key Scoring Changes

Starting with February 2025, ARR reviewers are asked to provide three scores: soundness, excitement, and overall recommendation.

## Soundness Score Requirements

Soundness scores must be justified by the text of the review. If you give a low Soundness score without finding any major faults, this means that your review is not a faithful explanation of your recommendation, and you need to revise it.

## Excitement vs. Soundness

Sometimes you may find the work sound, but not something that is interesting for you personally. Such preferences are more subjective and should be reflected in a separate excitement score, which is orthogonal to the assessment of the soundness of the paper.

## Area Chair Scoring

The ARR metareview score has changed in February 2025 to be more aligned with reviewer scores. Like with reviews, the score should be backed up by the meta-review text. The new options inclu

_source line 4580 · 2026-05-22T08:13:32.670445+00:00_

# ARR Reviewer Scoring Guidelines

## Scoring Dimensions

The ARR review form requires three distinct scores:

### 1. Soundness Score
This evaluates technical and methodological quality. The guidelines emphasize that "soundness scores must be justified by the text of the review." Low scores require explicit identification of flaws; vague justifications suggest unconscious bias rather than genuine technical concerns.

### 2. Excitement Score
This reflects personal interest orthogonal to quality assessment. The document notes this is "subjective and should be reflected in a separate excitement score, which is orthogonal to the assessment of the soundness." A paper can be technically sound yet personally unexciting, or vice versa.

### 3. Overall Assessment
This composite recommendation considers soundness, excitement, novelty, and impact together. It represents an explicit recommendation for conference acceptance, accommodating papers like efficiency improvements or resource development that may score lower on excitement but offer significant practical value.

## Scoring Principles

**Critical requirement:** Scores must align with review justifications. Unexplained low soundness scores indicate potential bias rather than legitimate criticism.

**Sound papers needn't be exciting.** High-quality work addressing niche topics or incremental improvements deserves consideration despite limited personal appeal.

The guidelines don't specify numerical scales (e.g., 1-5 or 1-10), focusing instead on conceptual coherence between assessment dimensions and written justification.

Based on the search results, I can provide you with information about the ACL ARR review form for 2025:

## Review Scoring System

Starting with February 2025, ARR reviewers are asked to provide three scores: soundness, excitement, and overall recommendation.

### Soundness Score
Soundness scores must be justified by the text of the review. If you give a low Soundness score without finding any major faults, this means that your review is not a faithful explanation of your recommendation, and you need to revise it. The soundness assessment evaluates whether the paper is sufficiently sound and thorough, clearly states scientific claims and provides adequate support for them.

# ACL Rolling Review Form - Complete Scoring Dimensions

## 1. **Reviewer Confidence**
- 5: "Positive that my evaluation is correct. I read the paper very carefully"
- 4: "Quite sure. I tried to check the important points carefully"
- 3: "Pretty sure, but there's a chance I missed something"
- 2: "Willing to defend my evaluation, but it is fairly likely that I missed some details"
- 1: "Not my area, or paper is very hard to understand"

## 2. **Soundness**
- 5: "Excellent: This study is one of the most thorough I have seen"
- 4.5: (intermediate)
- 4: "Strong: This study provides sufficient support for all of its claims"
- 3.5: (intermediate)
- 3: "Acceptable: This study provides sufficient support for its main claims"
- 2.5: (intermediate)
- 2: "Poor: Some of the main claims are not sufficiently supported"
- 1.5: (intermediate)
- 1: "Major Issues: This study is not yet sufficiently thorough to warrant publication"

## 3. **Excitement**
- 5: "Highly Exciting: I would recommend this paper to others"
- 4.5: (intermediate)
- 4: "Exciting: I would mention this paper to others"
- 3.5: (intermediate)
- 3: "Interesting: I might mention some points of this paper to others"
- 2.5: (intermediate)
- 2: "Potentially Interesting: this paper does not resonate with me"
- 1.5: (intermediate)
- 1: "Not Exciting: this paper does not resonate with me"

## 4. **Overall Assessment**
- 5: "Consider for Award: could be considered for outstanding paper award"
- 4.5: "Borderline Award"
- 4: "Conference: could be accepted to an *ACL conference"
- 3.5: "Borderline Conference"
- 3: "Findings: could be accepted to the Findings of the ACL"
- 2.5: "Borderline Findings"
- 2: "Resubmit next cycle: needs substantial revisions"
- 1.5: "Resubmit after next cycle: needs substantial revisions cannot be completed"
- 1: "Do not resubmit: has to be fully redone"

_source line 4654 · 2026-05-22T08:14:56.276193+00:00_

1	% Abstract — Depth Profiling of Recursive LLM Generation Chains
2	\begin{abstract}
3	As LLM outputs re-enter training corpora and seed further generation, understanding how recursive generation reshapes text distributions becomes essential for data provenance and corpus curation---yet existing detectors answer only the binary human-or-machine question, ignoring \emph{generation depth}: we introduce \emph{depth profiling} to address this gap.
4	We decompose distributional drift under recursive generation into location and shape components, revealing an asymmetry we term \emph{differential depth response} (DDR): across eight base-model generators from five families, NLL location features drift monotonically with depth ($d{=}0.76$--$1.17$) while three shape features remain near-stationary ($d < 0.10$), with entropy std intermediate ($d{=}0.55$).
5	Prior work documents \emph{what converges}; the central finding here is \emph{what is approximately preserved}: shape invariance provides a cross-generator reference that enables coarse ordinal depth classification (gen-0\,/\,gen-1\,/\,gen-2+) without per-generator calibration, achieving leak-free binary F1\,=\,0.694 under leave-one-generator-out evaluation; an end-to-end classifier reaches 3-class F1\,=\,0.905 (6-gen LOGO; Appendix~\ref{app:qlora}).
6	Instruction tuning compresses DDR, bounding current applicability to base models.
7	\end{abstract}
8

1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	An estimated 7--17\% of recent scientific publications already contain substantial LLM-modified content \cite{mapping_llm_science,monitoring_ai_review}. As these outputs re-enter training corpora \cite{dolma,fineweb} and seed further generation, text accumulates at varying \emph{generation depths}; each successive cycle reshapes distributional properties in ways that matter for data provenance, corpus auditing, and contamination monitoring \cite{ccnet,dsir} as well as model collapse analysis \cite{curse_of_recursion,strong_model_collapse}. Understanding \emph{how} recursive generation alters text distributions---and which properties change versus which are preserved---is therefore a prerequisite for principled corpus curation, yet current methods address only binary human-or-machine detection \cite{detectgpt,fast_detectgpt,binoculars,ld_score}.
5	Base models provide the cleanest substrate to isolate depth effects from alignment-induced changes.
6	We find that recursive generation produces a decomposable depth profile: distributional \emph{location} drifts with depth while \emph{shape} remains stable across generators, an asymmetry sufficient for cross-generator depth prediction.
7	
8	Distributional compression under recursive generation is well-documented \cite{curious_decline,broken_telephone,markovian_chains}, yet existing analyses characterize \emph{that} and \emph{why} convergence occurs without decomposing what drifts from what is preserved.
9	We introduce \emph{depth profiling}, classifying text by ordinal generation depth (gen-0\,/\,gen-1\,/\,gen-2+), and decompose this compression into structurally distinct components: across eight base-model generators spanning five architectural families (0.6B--8B), the NLL distribution's central tendency (mean entropy (reference-model NLL), lexical diversity) drifts monotonically with depth while its shape (entropy extremes, tail structure) remains an order of magni
…[truncated 2588 chars]

1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related}
3	
4	\subsection{AI-Generated Text Detection}
5	\label{sec:related-detection}
6	
7	The detection literature spans statistical tests \cite{gltr,grover,release_strategies}, zero-shot scoring \cite{detectgpt,fast_detectgpt,binoculars,dna_gpt}, surprisal diversity \cite{diveye}, spectral analysis of relative likelihood \cite{xu_spectrum_detection}, geometric invariants \cite{intrinsic_dim_detection}, and learned classifiers \cite{ghostbuster,feat_aug_transformer,reliable_detection,ld_score}; see \citet{wu_survey_2025} and \citet{jawahar_survey} for surveys.
8	RAID \cite{raid} benchmarks binary detection across generators and attacks; we extend the task from binary to ordinal depth estimation.
9	Several recent efforts move beyond the binary boundary (collaboration roles \cite{beyond_binary}, joint detection-attribution \cite{two_birds}, creator-editor modes \cite{beyond_final_actor}, multi-generator attribution \cite{semeval2024_task8}) but all taxonomize by \emph{source} or \emph{production mode}, not recursive generation \emph{depth}; repurposing their scores as depth predictors reveals high cross-generator variance (binary F1\,=\,0.54--0.71; Table~\ref{tab:baselines}) \cite{paraphrase_evades,detection_possibilities}.
10	Our contribution is orthogonal: ordinal depth classification \cite{gutierrez_ordinal_survey,diaz_soft_labels_ordinal,coral_ordinal,kasa_ordinality_nlp} as a task, and the finding that combining drifting and stationary features yields consistent cross-generator performance where individual detection scores show high variance \cite{uchendu_attribution,munir_looking_glass}.
11	
12	\subsection{Model Collapse and Iterative Generation}
13	\label{sec:related-collapse}
14	
15	Self-consuming generative models progressively lose distributional coverage \cite{self_consuming_mad,curse_of_recursion,briesch_self_consuming,seddik_synthetic_data}, though data mixing mitigates collapse \cite{model_collapse_avoidable,strong
…[truncated 3983 chars]

1	\section{Method}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	\begin{table}[t!]
5	\centering
6	\footnotesize
7	\caption{Seven distributional features. ``Entropy'' features are computed as per-token NLL under Pythia-410M (see \S\ref{sec:features}); surface features require no reference model. DDR analysis (\S\ref{sec:results-differential}) reveals that entropy mean and the two surface features (TTR, hapax ratio) drift with depth while entropy max, min, and tail decay remain near-stationary; entropy std is intermediate.}
8	\label{tab:features}
9	\begin{tabular}{llp{3.8cm}}
10	\toprule
11	\textbf{Feature} & \textbf{Source} & \textbf{Measures} \\
12	\midrule
13	\texttt{entropy\_mean} & Ref.\ NLL & Central tendency of token surprise \\
14	\texttt{entropy\_std}  & Ref.\ NLL & Spread of token surprise \\
15	\texttt{entropy\_max}  & Ref.\ NLL & Maximum token surprise \\
16	\texttt{entropy\_min}  & Ref.\ NLL & Minimum token surprise \\
17	\texttt{tail\_decay}   & Ref.\ NLL & Log-prob decay slope (ranks 100--1K) \\
18	\midrule
19	\texttt{ttr}           & Surface & Type-token ratio \\
20	\texttt{hapax\_ratio}  & Surface & Proportion of hapax legomena \\
21	\bottomrule
22	\end{tabular}
23	\end{table}
24	
25	\subsection{Task Formulation}
26	\label{sec:task}
27	
28	Given a text $x$, we predict its \emph{generation depth} $d \in \{0, 1, 2{+}\}$: human-written ($d{=}0$), first-generation LLM output ($d{=}1$), or text produced by two or more recursive LLM generations ($d{=}2{+}$).
29	A generation chain of depth $k$ is defined recursively: $x^{(0)} \sim \mathcal{C}_0$ (human corpus), $x^{(k)} = G(x^{(k-1)})$ for $k \geq 1$, where $G$ denotes stochastic autoregressive generation.
30	Depths beyond 2 are merged into $2{+}$ because feature convergence compresses inter-class margins at deeper levels (\S\ref{sec:results-differential}).
31	
32	\subsection{Generation Chain Construction}
33	\label{sec:generation}
34	
35	We construct generation chains using eight generators spanning five model families: \textb

1	\section{Results}
2	\label{sec:results}
3	
4	\subsection{Depth Classification Performance}
5	\label{sec:results-classification}
6	
7	The 7-feature ensemble achieves 3-class LOGO F1\,=\,0.762 (95\% CI [0.758, 0.766]; $N$\,=\,37{,}058) across eight generators from five families (Table~\ref{tab:logo-results}), with cross-generator F1 spread of 9.1\,pp capturing generator selection uncertainty.
8	Because gen-0 seeds are shared across generators (\S\ref{sec:generation}), gen-0 classification is near-perfect (per-class F1\,$\approx$\,0.94), inflating the 3-class metric.
9	We therefore adopt \textbf{leak-free binary F1} (gen-1 vs.\ gen-2+)\,=\,0.694 (CI [0.688, 0.700]) as the primary metric throughout: it excludes gen-0 entirely and is unaffected by seed sharing.
10	Per-generator binary F1 ranges from 0.633 (Mistral-7B) to 0.724 (Pythia-2.8B; SD\,=\,0.028), with the primary difficulty concentrated at the gen-1\,/\,gen-2+ boundary (Appendix~\ref{app:confusion}).
11	CIs reflect bootstrap sampling variability; the cross-generator range (9.1\,pp) captures the distinct and larger uncertainty of generator selection.
12	
13	\paragraph{Cross-model generalization.}
14	Cross-model chains (randomly selected generator per step) yield binary F1\,=\,0.830 (95\% CI [.818, .841]; Appendix~\ref{app:cross-model-detail}) under 5-generator LOGO.\footnote{Pythia-2.8B/1B, OLMo-2-1B, Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B; generator-level LOGO with bootstrap CIs ($n$\,=\,2{,}000). Not directly comparable to 8-generator LOGO due to differing subsets.}
15	Single-generator transfer (Appendix~\ref{app:transfer}) confirms the signal is not a mixed-chain artifact (mean transfer F1\,=\,0.810; LOFO\,=\,0.686); the cross-model advantage is only 2\,pp.
16	A scale effect emerges: Pythia achieves the highest F1 (0.718--0.724) while larger models degrade (Mistral-7B: 0.633), partly reflecting same-family affinity with the Pythia-410M reference (cross-reference spread $\leq$0.013; \S\ref{sec:results-differential}); cross-domain s

1	\section{Discussion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	%% Mechanism — why DDR exists
5	We hypothesize that the differential depth response reflects a structural property of nucleus sampling: top-$p$ truncation preserves the conditional distribution's functional form while recursive inputs grow increasingly self-typical, compressing expected NLL monotonically (Appendix~\ref{app:nll-distribution}). DDR is an empirical decomposition validated by converging evidence; the nucleus sampling hypothesis offers a testable but unproven mechanistic explanation.
6	Two testable predictions follow---increasing randomness should weaken location drift, semantic constraints should attenuate it---both confirmed: at $\tau{=}1.0$, depth--NLL correlation collapses ($\rho{=}{-}0.002$; Appendix~\ref{app:sampling-sensitivity}); elaboration prompts reduce F1 to 0.730 (Appendix~\ref{app:prompt-type}). Drifting features contribute $5.5\times$ more (Appendix~\ref{app:ddr-causality}).
7	Shape stability holds even when drift is suppressed: at $\tau{=}1.0$ ($d < 0.15$; Appendix~\ref{app:ddr-temp-stability}), under greedy decoding (mean $|d|{=}0.05$), and with top-$k$ ($k{=}50$; Appendix~\ref{app:decoding-strategy}).
8	
9	%% Implications — what DDR enables
10	Depth constitutes a new operational dimension for corpus management that binary human-vs-machine classifiers cannot access.
11	The classifier supports batch contamination estimation (${\pm}3$--$4$\% at $n{=}1{,}000$; Appendix~\ref{app:error-analysis}) rather than per-document filtering; depth-stratified curation may serve as a prophylactic against model collapse \cite{drayson_detection_prevents_collapse}, though preliminary evidence shows no downstream improvement (limited power, $n{=}3$ seeds; Appendix~\ref{app:downstream}).
12	
13	%% Scope \& Connections — where DDR works and fails
14	DDR is mechanistically distinct from model collapse \cite{curse_of_recursion,strong_model_collapse}: retraining erodes tail coverage via weight updates, whereas 
…[truncated 783 chars]

1	\section*{Limitations}
2	\label{sec:limitations}
3	
4	All eight generators are base (pretrained) models with $\leq$8.2B parameters.
5	Instruction-tuned variants show substantially degraded depth signal (Appendix~\ref{app:instruct}), and Gemma-2-2B exhibits near-zero gen-1 F1 due to tokenizer and attention differences (Appendix~\ref{app:9gen-scale}), indicating that architecture-specific generalization failures can occur; frontier models (70B+) remain untested.
6	
7	The evaluation assumes a fixed decoding regime (temperature 0.7, nucleus $p{=}0.9$) with English Wikipedia seeds and an English-centric reference model (Pythia-410M); cross-temperature transfer degrades at mismatched settings (Appendix~\ref{app:cross-temp}), and non-English applicability is untested.
8	
9	Depth resolution is bounded at three classes (gen-0\,/\,gen-1\,/\,gen-2+); finer-grained 5-class classification yields F1\,$\approx$\,0.50 with deeper depths mutually indistinguishable (Appendix~\ref{app:finer-depth}).
10

1	\section*{Ethics Statement}
2	\label{sec:ethics}
3	
4	All texts analyzed in this work are LLM-generated from publicly available Wikipedia seeds; no personal or sensitive data is involved.
5	Our method estimates the generation depth of a text passage (how many recursive generation steps it has undergone), not the identity of its author.
6	Individual authorship attribution is outside both the scope and the capability of this approach; privacy implications are minimal.
7	We acknowledge that depth estimation tools could in principle be used to selectively suppress machine-generated text in contexts where such content is legitimate (e.g., accessibility tools, assistive writing).
8	We encourage deployment in data governance pipelines where provenance transparency, rather than content removal, is the objective.
9	All code, data, and trained models will be released upon publication.
10	
11	\paragraph{Licenses.}
12	All generator models are publicly available under permissive licenses (Apache 2.0 for Pythia, OLMo, and Mistral; Qwen License for Qwen3; Llama Community License for Llama 3.2).
13	Wikipedia seed passages are distributed under CC BY-SA 3.0.
14	
15	\paragraph{Compute budget.}
16	All experiments were conducted on 4$\times$ NVIDIA RTX 5090 GPUs (32\,GB each), totaling approximately 62 GPU-hours.
17	
18	\paragraph{Software.}
19	Experiments used Python 3.12.3, PyTorch 2.10.0 (CUDA 12.8), Transformers 4.57.6, XGBoost 3.2.0, scikit-learn 1.8.0, NumPy 2.2.6, pandas 3.0.2, and SciPy 1.17.1.
20

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	Recursive generation in base models produces a decomposable depth response---location drifts while shape is approximately preserved---enabling cross-generator depth classification (leak-free binary F1\,=\,0.694; 3-class: 0.905) across eight generators from five families, establishing depth as an analytical axis complementary to model-collapse analyses.
5	Extending DDR to instruction-tuned and frontier-scale models remains the key open challenge.
6

特异性。Appendix z.3 的 Granger causality 是一步进展但也仅测相关性。\n- 建议：将 \"both confirmed\" 改为 \"both consistent with the hypothesis\"；明确承认替代解释。\n\n**W2** [Warning] 与并发工作 Geng et al. Markovian、Mohamed et al. information loss）收敛到 ≈3 classes 的意义——这种 convergence 强化了 3-class ceiling 作为 fundamental property 的论点。\n\n**W3** [Warning] Ordinal classification 文献连接薄弱\n- 具体位置：Section 2.1 classification/regression 文献的讨论仅有一个括号引用（Gutierrez et al. ordinal survey）。Ordinal 方法在 Appendix h 中表现与 XGBoost 相当（Table 12），暗示 ordinal structure 对当前 3-class setup 帮助有限——这本身是一个值得在 main text 中讨论的发现。\n- 建议：在 Related Work 或 Discussion 中增加 1-2 句讨论为何 ordinal methods 未带来增益（可能因为只有 3 个 class，ordinal 优势在 fine-grained 设置中才体现）。\n\n**W4** [Warning] Abstract 中 F1=0.905 来自 6-generator LOGO 而非 8-generator\n- 具体位置：Abstract (line 5: \"an end-to-end classifier reaches 3-class F1 = 0.905 (6-gen LOGO; Appendix e)\")\n- 问题：Abstract 同时报告 binary F1=0.694（8-gen LOGO）和 3-class F1=0.905（6-gen LOGO），但 reader 可能不注意 \"(6-gen LOGO)\" 的限定。6-gen LOGO 是原始 6 generator 子集的结果，不是完整 8-generator 评估。\n- 建议：在 Abstract 中更显式地标注差异，或统一使用 8-gen LOGO 结果。\n\n**W5** [Warning] \"Entropy\" 术语可能造成混淆\n- 具体位置：Section 3.3 Terminology paragraph\n- 问题：论文明确说明 \"entropy\" 指 reference-model NLL，不是 information-theoretic entropy。但这一声明仅出现在 Section 3.3 的一个段落中，之后全文持续使用 \"entropy_mean\" 等术语。对快速阅读的 reviewer/reader 而言，可能误以为这是 true entropy。\n- 建议：考虑在 Table 1 的 caption 中再次提醒；或在 Figure 2/Figure 1 的 axis label 中标注 \"(ref-model NLL)\"。\n\n**W6** [Warning] Figure 1 在灰度打印下可读性有限\n- 具体位置：Figure 1 (p.6)\n- 问题：Figure 1 使用颜色区分 drifting/intermediate/stationary zones，marker shape 用于 grayscale accessibility（caption 说明）。但 7 个 feature × 8 个 generator 的数据点密集，在灰度下区分仍然困难。\n- 建议：考虑在 Figure 1 中增加 zone label 或分面（facet）设计。\n\n---\n\n## 小问题（不影响接收）\n\n- Section 4.1: \"pooled F1 is slightly higher than per-generator macro F1 in Table 2\"（Table 3 caption）——这一 distinction 对 reader 可能不直观，建议在首次出现时解释清楚。\n- Table 2 和 Table 3 使用不同的 F1 计算方式（per-generator macro vs. pooled），增加了比较的认知负担。\n- Ethics Statement: \"PyTorch 2.10.0 (CUDA 12.8)\" —— 请确认版本号准确（截至 2026 年 5 月，PyTorch 2.10 是否已发布）。\n- 附录非常详尽（25+ sections，25 pages），但部分 section 之间有内容重复（如 DDR validation 出现在多处）。考虑合并或交叉引用以减少冗余。\n- Appendix 标签使用自定义 alphalph 方案（a, b, c, ...），当 section 超过 26 个时可能出现问题；目前看来尚未溢出。\n\n---\n\n## 主要优势\n\n1. **任务定义的原创性**：Depth profiling 作为 ordinal classification（vs. binary detection）是真正的新任务定义，有明确的实际动机（data provenance, corpus curation）。与现有 AI text detection 的区别清晰且有说服力。\n\n2. **DDR 分解是高质量的实证发现**：Location drift vs. shape stability 的不对称性通过 5 种 effect-size 指标（Cohen's d, Glass's Δ, Cliff's δ, KS, CV shift）、k-means 聚类验证（silhouette=0.660）、per-generator replication（8/8 generators 独立恢复相同 partition）、TOST 等价性检验多维度验证。这种 methodological rigor 值得肯定。\n\n3. **实验设计的严谨性**：LOGO cross-validation 是最严格的 generalization protocol；seed-sharing control（F1=0.680）、length confound control（F1=0.724）、cross-reference model validation（spread ≤ 0.013）、independent-seed experiment 等控制实验系统性地排除了主要 confounds。\n\n4. **诚实的 scope characterization**：论文坦诚地报告了 instruction tuning 的失败（Appendix k）、Gemma-2-2B anomaly（Appendix z.5）、下游任务的 negative result（Appendix m）、以及 high-temperature 下的性能下降。这种 honesty 在投稿论文中不常见。\n\n5. **与并发工作的恰当处理**：Related Work（Section 2.2）和 Appendix z.6（Table 46）对 Mohamed et al.、Geng et al.、Wang et al. 进行了结构化比较，清晰区分了各自的 scope 和贡献。\n\n6. **附录的实验覆盖度极高**：25+ 附录 section 覆盖了 temperature sensitivity、prompt type、domain transfer、perturbation robustness、instruction tuning、QLoRA、finer depth、feature ablation、confusion matrix、error analysis、downstream utility、cross-temperature transfer、DDR causality、decoding strategy、9-generator scale 等。这种 thoroughness 大幅提升了论文的可信度。\n\n---\n\n## Questions to Authors\n\n1. DDR 在 instruction-tuned models 上的崩溃是否主要由 RLHF/DPO 引起的 distribution shift 导致？是否测试过仅做 SFT（无 reward modeling）的模型？\n\n2. 3-class ceiling 是否在更大的特征集（如 full NLL distribution 的 non-parametric statistics、n-gram level features）下仍然成立？\n\n3. 对于 Mistral-7B 性能最低（binary F1=0.633）的现象，除了 \"larger models degrade\" 之外是否有更具体的分析？是 family-specific 还是 scale-specific 的效应？\n\n4. Cross-model chains 的 F1 提升（+2pp over single-generator LOGO）是否因为 mixed chains 引入了 generator diversity 从而更接近 training distribution？\n\n---\n\n## Missing References（建议补充）\n\n- 无明确遗漏的 critical references。Related Work 对 AI text detection、model collapse、vocabulary diversity、data provenance 四个领域的覆盖较为完整。\n- 可考虑补充的方向：(1) 更多 ordinal classification 在 NLP 中的应用（如 sentiment ordinal classification）；(2) 2025-2026 年的 data contamination detection 工作（如果有新进展）。\n\n---\n\n## 论文定位\n\n这是一篇 well-executed empirical study，DDR 分解是一个 solid contribution。但 base-model-only 的限制使其实际 impact 受限，0.694 binary F1 不构成 strong practical tool。综合考虑：\n- **如果 N1 被充分 address**（在 Introduction 更早限定 scope + Discussion 中充分讨论 instruct 失效机制），论文适合 **EMNLP Findings**（Overall 3.0-3.5）。\n- 如果能补充 instruct model 的 DDR 分析（即使是 negative result 的 structured analysis），有潜力升至 **Borderline Conference**（3.5）。\n- 当前状态：**3.5 Borderline Conference**——主要因为 DDR 发现的质量和实验 rigor 足够高，但 practical scope 是 concern。",
